Your job: after 24 h, audit its paper trades: n, per £10, hit rate, ticks, by price band/venue/secs-to-off; check fills are 'through' not 'touch'; check no in-play entries; standard verdict. Original spec: **Racing pre-off scalper, Caan Berry / Peter Webb rules, PAPER on the live stream.** Build /root/pe-race/preoff_scalp_paper.py in the style of /root/pe-race/race_mm_size.py (read it first for the stream_ticks access pattern, heartbeat and keepalive conventions). Rules, exact: UK/IE WIN markets only, last 10 minutes before the off, total matched >= £50k at entry, runner price 2.0–8.0, book spread <= 2 ticks. Weight of money over a rolling 30 s on the first three price points each side: when back-side money / (back+lay) >= 0.65 and the last 3 traded prices are not lengthening, BACK at the touch; when <= 0.35 and not shortening, LAY at the touch. Exit: +2 ticks (lay back out / back back out) or −2 ticks stop, or 60 s elapsed, or 30 s before the off, whichever first; one position per runner at a time, max 3 open positions, never in play. Fills: paper fill only when the touch price trades THROUGH your level (not touched). Table preoff_scalp_paper (ts, market_id, venue, sel, side, entry_px, exit_px, exit_reason, ticks, pnl_per_10), heartbeat preoff_scalp_heartbeat, keepalive via nohup. No orders of any kind. Report after 24 h: n, per £10, hit rate, by price band and by venue.

Bundesliga sit within about 1 point; but those lines were picked on both seasons, so they cannot prove themselves. Rule: for every league on the tape, overround-removed Under 2.5 at the first print v the result, as in 499; rank correlation of the 2024 error against the 2025 error across leagues; then, choosing on 2024 only, lay Under 2.5 in leagues with error <= −2 points and back it in leagues >= +2 points, and trade 2025 only, one bet per game, one tick adverse. A single season cannot meet the two-half rule, so split 2025 by median date. If 2024 does not predict 2025, the Segunda lead is selection and is closed. LTP only, so a pass is an upper bound and then needs the real touch on liq_sample.

DONE 2026-11-05 — nothing passes, 0 of 84; best Cricket X10 FADE P<1.5 n 76 +0.049 t 0.13; reversion +0.095 to +0.332 v round trip -0.72 to -1.08 (reports/finder_2026-11-05_item126.md) (Finder's own, source d; table bf_hist_other, MATCH_ODDS, Cricket and Tennis, jobs/499_preoff_shock.sh) **Pre-off price shock, cricket (the toss) and tennis: follow or fade, closed before the off.** Rationale: pre-off momentum was tested for football, racing and greyhounds, never for these two, and a cricket toss is a public shock at about T-30; closing pre-off needs no result, so no final-print inference. Rule fixed before any price was read: scheduled market_time = T, no look at the real off. Trigger: first pre-off print in T-90 to T-5 at least X% (3, 5, 10) away from the same runner's latest print 30 s to 5 min earlier. One entry per market, first trigger. Entry: the next print of that runner within 120 s, must be pre-off, worse of trigger and next, one tick adverse, price 1.10-20, no next print = no trade, counted. FOLLOW backs a shortener and lays a drifter, FADE the reverse. Exit: first print 10+ min after entry and pre-off, else the last pre-off print after entry, else the first in-play print, one tick adverse, 2% commission on the green. No exit print = counted and reported. Lines: sport x X x side x (all, T-90/-45, T-45/-15, T-15/-5, price under 1.5, 1.5-2.5, 2.5-20), 84, plus a control at the first print after T-30. Halves by median date. The Draw in cricket left out.

Entry and guards as jobs/499_team_form_stage.sh: earliest real print in tape minutes 0..2, lag outside -5..+20 min v the kick-off dropped and counted, goal known before the print dropped and counted, one tick adverse, 2% commission, £10 risk, hold to FT, so nothing drops at settlement. States, UTC: HOUR <12, 12-14, 15-17, 18-19, 20+; DOW Mon, Tue-Thu, Fri, Sat, Sun; MONTH Aug-Sep, Oct-Nov, Dec-Feb, Mar-May, Jun-Jul; SLOT ODD3 (3+ h from the league's commonest kick-off hour last season, 50+ games last season), USUAL (under 1 h). Selections HOME, DRAW, AWAY, OVER, UNDER 2.5, back and lay: 17 x 5 x 2 = 170 lines, plus an ALL control not counted as a line.

Part of the give-back is prints bouncing between back and lay, which is why it needs the real book. Earlier resting tests on football pre-match failed on fills (492, 494, 496), so read as a long shot. Rule fixed now: X10 only, trigger as item 126, rest one tick inside the touch on the fade side for 120 s, count a fill only on a print through the level, exit 10 min later crossing the real touch, unfilled = no trade and counted.

Control, not counted as a line: same band, previous goal >=20 min earlier. Halves by season; t by game also required.

DONE 2026-11-05 — nothing passes (jobs/499_racing_preoff_tape.sh, reports/finder_2026-11-05_items169-171.md; 27,034 races, 0 of 40, all negative in both halves; best EARLY DRIFT <3 FOLLOW n 331 -0.128 t -1.98 h1 -0.208 h2 -0.048) (Finder's own, source b, Caan Berry; table bf_hist_other Horse Racing WIN, GB and IE, Sep 2024-Sep 2026, from jobs/499_greyhound_momentum.sh) **Racing pre-off follow and fade on the two-year tape, closed before the off.** Rationale: horses have only had a 3-day pilot on stream_ticks (items 53, 0c, 90 races); greyhounds had item 58. Rule fixed before prices are read. Early window: reference = last print T-45 to T-20, signal = last print T-15 to T-10; late window: reference = last print T-20 to T-10, signal = last print T-8 to T-5. SHORT signal/reference <= 0.90, DRIFT >= 1.10, FLAT 0.98-1.02 is a control, not gated. Entry = first print after the signal print, by T-5 (early) or T-3 (late), worse of it and the next print within 120 s, one tick adverse; signal print is never the entry, so bounce cannot make the signal. Exit = last pre-off print after entry, one tick adverse. Entry is the runner's last pre-off print: settle at win_lose from racing_bsp, else charge -£10. Bands on the entry print: <3, 3-6, 6-12, 12-20, ALL<=20. One entry per race per class per band: largest move, ties by name. FOLLOW = back SHORT, lay DRIFT; FADE = lay SHORT, back DRIFT. 40 gated lines, halves by date median, house bar.

Rule fixed before prices are read. Entry = last pre-off print T-5 to T-1 of the shortest runner in the band, one per race per band, one tick adverse. Rule fixed before prices are read. Favourite = shorter last pre-off print, 1.30-2.00. T2: first in-play print above 2.00 and at most 15. T3: first in-play print >= 1.20 x either of the two prints before it, with that earlier print at most 15. Entry = next print, never the trigger's own, one tick adverse. Back and lay the favourite. T2 holds to the real result; T3 exits at the first print at or under the pre-move price within 40 minutes, one tick adverse, else holds to the result. Bands 1.30-1.50, 1.50-2.00, ALL. One entry per match per rule. Walkovers void. No results match = not scored, counted. 12 gated lines, halves by date median, house bar.

DONE 2026-11-06 — nothing passes, 0 of 8; best DNB G2 CHEAP back n 179 +2.078 t 2.66 h1 +1.816 h2 +2.416; DNB G2 DEAR lay n 279 +1.229 t 1.70; follow-ups items 264, 265; reports/finder_2026-11-06_items257-265.md (jobs/499_derivative_gap.sh; Finder's own, source c; tables bf_hist_other, bf_hist_result; items 111, 113, 157, 164 read these markets alone, never against Match Odds on official results) **Draw No Bet and Double Chance against the price Match Odds implies.** Rationale: both are sums of Match Odds outcomes and thinner, and the 3 Nov lag read says DNB closes only about 11% of its gap a minute. Rule fixed before prices: every month with official results, one market per event per type, last non-in-play print T-20 to T-2 on all three MO runners and the derivative runner, each MO print within 600 s of the derivative print, MO book 0.98 to 1.08. Fair from MO normalised: DNB home = H/(H+A); Home or Draw = H+D; Draw or Away = D+A; Home or Away = H+A. Gap g = fair less 1/print. Per game per line, the runner with the largest gap. G2 = 0.02 to 0.04, G4 = 0.04 to 0.10. CHEAP (g > 0) back, DEAR (g < 0) lay: 2 markets x 2 bands x 2 = 8 scored lines. Worse of the print and the next within 120 s, one tick adverse, 2%, £10 risk, to the official result. A drawn game voids DNB: no result row there and MO's winner the Draw counts as a trade at £0, never dropped; other missing rows dropped and counted. Standard bar, halves by date median. Cannot pass, shown: G10 (g 0.10 or more, stale), the same bands with the sides swapped, NEAR (gap under 0.01), and the taker's fills only (back on a down-print, lay on an up-print).

DONE 2026-11-06 — pilot, a taker cannot have it: 0 of 130 games show the 2 points at the touch, median DNB spread 4.51 points, 29 of 260 runners show it at mid; only 130 of 1,215 games had a usable row; reports/finder_2026-11-06_items257-265.md (jobs/499_dnb_touch.sh; Finder's own, source a; table liq_sample, local copy, about 6 days) **Item 263's DNB gap at the real touch: is the 2 points there to take?** Rationale: 263 DNB G2 CHEAP back n 179 +2.078 t 2.66 h1 +1.816 h2 +2.416, DEAR lay n 279 +1.229 t 1.70, both on prints; the 3 Nov read said the half-spread is wider than the gap. A pilot that cannot pass on 6 days. Rule fixed before these prices are read: MO and DNB, last row at minute 0 at least 120 s before the game's first row at minute 1 or later, all three MO runners and both DNB runners two-sided within 120 s of each other. Fair from MO mids normalised, book 0.98 to 1.08. CHEAP = fair less 1/back_px >= 0.02, back at back_px; DEAR = 1/lay_px less fair >= 0.02, lay at lay_px; each no more than 0.10; largest gap per game per side. One tick adverse, 2%, £10 risk. Result from MO's last row: one runner's ltp <= 1.1 wins; the Draw voids DNB at £0, counted as a trade; no clear winner is unresolved, counted, never scored. Shown: how many games show the gap at mid against at the touch, and the median DNB spread in points. Halves by date.

412. DONE 2026-11-07 — nothing passes, pilot; 37 fills from about 49,600 quoted snapshots, LAYQ K2 n 10 -0.231, BACKQ K2 n 13 -1.313, all four negative; fills inferred from sampled prints, so it reads the worst fills, not a real fill rate; jobs/499_ht_quote_hedge.sh, reports/finder_2026-11-07_items407-412.md — (Finder's own, source a, from items 410 and 411; local liq_sample HTFT, HT_MO, MO; job 499_ht_quote_hedge; pilot on about 2 days, n unlikely to reach the bar) **Quote inside the wide HT/FT book after half-time, hedge on Match Odds when filled.** Rationale: item 411, HT/FT spread median 6.94 points v 0.85 on MO; item 410, a print off MO does not carry news, G2 CHEAP won 0.427 v implied MO 0.437 v implied HT/FT 0.396, G2 DEAR 0.507 v 0.483 v 0.538, so whoever rests on the far side of those prints looks paid; takers cannot get it, item 411's locks are 18 of 16,898 snapshots. Item 62's resting greyhound idea and item 49's wide-book quotes had no hedge market; this has one. Rule fixed before any fill is summed: X as item 411, minute 52 or later, X/F on HTFT and F on MO on the same ts. LAYQ: offer a lay at K ticks under the MO back, only if that is inside the HT/FT book, under the HT/FT lay and over the HT/FT back. BACKQ: offer a back at K ticks over the MO lay, only if inside the HT/FT book. K 2 and 4. Re-quoted at each snapshot. Filled only if the next snapshot, at most 120 s on, shows a new HT/FT ltp strictly through the level, under it for LAYQ and over it for BACKQ, so queue position is not assumed. Hedged on MO at that next snapshot's touch, back for LAYQ and lay for BACKQ; no MO book there, no hedge, closed at the next MO touch in 120 s, else counted at the result-free worst of -£10. P&L is the worse of the two outcomes after 2% on the winning market only, £10 on the HT/FT leg. First fill per runner per game. A goal between the snapshots is left in as a bad hedge, conservative as Betfair lapses most orders at suspension. Lines LAYQ and BACKQ at K 2, 4; standard bar, halves by date, t by game. Printed: quotes offered, fill rate, median hedge slippage, losers. Even a pass earns a paper quote test with real fills, as fills here are inferred from sampled prints.

Rule fixed now: re-run each job unchanged, adding only a split by whether the entry print has moved at least 20% from the runner's last print in the 6 match minutes before the event, in the direction the event should move it; a lead whose profit is only in the unmoved prints is struck and its queue line marked so. Not a money line itself; it stops the next goal-entry lead fooling us.

436. TODO (Finder's own, source a, from item 339; tables liq_sample, xx_ip_331, both local; job 499_xx_unconfirmed) **Fade a Betfair in-play jump the other book does not confirm.** Rationale: item 339, after a 5% Betfair move the other book was still unmoved at its next row on only 12-17% of signals, and taking its stale touch lost (Matchbook n 639 -1.496, Smarkets n 658 -1.459), so either the thin book is slow or Betfair overshot; this asks the second question on Betfair alone, where the spread is tight. Rule fixed now, before any price is read: football match odds, minute 5 to 85, each book alone; Betfair mid moved 5% or more between consecutive two-sided rows of one runner up to 120 s apart; the other book's mid (last row at or before each Betfair row, within 60 s) moved under 0.5% over that span AND its next row, within 120 s, is still within 0.5% (UNCONFIRMED); CONFIRMED (other moved 2% or more the same way by its next row) is the control and cannot pass. Entry at Betfair's first row at or after the other book's next row, FADE (mid rose: back at back-1 tick; mid fell: lay at lay+1 tick) and FOLLOW (the reverse) as two lines; exit at the first Betfair row at or after entry+300 s, one tick adverse, greened; no later row: settled at the last prints. Over 50 not traded. Replay guard total_matched >= 0.999 of the market high. First signal per class per runner per game. Standard bar, halves by date, t by game too. Suspended other books may still print unchanged rows, so a pass needs a goal_timeline check before any stake.
